   Scope:
   The task explicitly requires committing all current changes.; The target file is not yet tracked and the task requires adding it along with other files.

   Check:
   Run `git show --stat HEAD` or `git status` to confirm the commit touches exactly the intended file and that unrelated modifications remain unstaged.

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Confirm the user's scoping: v1 needs no pagination — messages already use scroll+prefetch and group members are capped at 200; only add RPC/keyset pagination if/when an Inbox conversation-list UI is actually built.
  2. Prefer chaining shell commands with `;` on PowerShell; `&&` fails with 'The token && is not a valid statement separator'.
  3. Prefer reporting the actual branch name when it is not main/trunk, so the user knows where the commit landed.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid `git add .` or `git add -A` when the tree has unrelated modifications — it would sweep them into the commit.
  2. Avoid assuming `&&` works in the shell; the first attempt failed with a PowerShell parser error before being retried with `;`.
  3. Avoid claiming success without a post-commit `git show`/`git status` check that the commit touched exactly one file.

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Confirm the user's scoping: v1 needs no pagination — messages already use scroll+prefetch and group members are capped at 200; only add RPC/keyset pagination if/when an Inbox conversation-list UI is actually built.
  2. Prefer chaining shell commands with `;` on PowerShell; `&&` fails with 'The token && is not a valid statement separator'.
  3. Prefer reporting the actual branch name when it is not main/trunk, so the user knows where the commit landed.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid `git add .` or `git add -A` when the tree has unrelated modifications — it would sweep them into the commit.
  2. Avoid assuming `&&` works in the shell; the first attempt failed with a PowerShell parser error before being retried with `;`.
  3. Avoid claiming success without a post-commit `git show`/`git status` check that the commit touched exactly one file.

2. Candidate Experience (unverified)
   id: policy_d74021747df4bd1724bf

   Use:
   Candidate, unverified guidance. Treat it as a hypothesis and verify it in the current task before use.

   Trigger:
   A task requires creating or modifying a specific file and committing it, while the working tree already contains unrelated modifications.

   Guidance:
   1. Write the target file with the exact required content. 2. Stage only that file using `git add <target-path>`. 3. Commit with `git commit -m '<message>'`. 4. If the shell rejects the chained command (e.g., PowerShell parsing error), run the `git add` and `git commit` as separate commands.

   Scope:
   The task explicitly requires committing all current changes.; The target file is not yet tracked and the task requires adding it along with other files.

   Check:
   Run `git show --stat HEAD` or `git status` to confirm the commit touches exactly the intended file and that unrelated modifications remain unstaged.

3. Experience
   id: policy_5f2494caf8d5db934d2c

   Use:
   Use as prior successful guidance when the current task matches.

   Trigger:
   User asks for test coverage numbers for a project, and the coverage artifact (e.g. lcov.info) may be stale, partial, or absent at decision time.

   Guidance:
   Run the project's full test suite with coverage enabled so the coverage artifact is regenerated, then parse that artifact to aggregate total line coverage (covered/total lines) and a per-area breakdown.

   Avoid:
   Recommending extra scope (read-only inbox, watchConversations/loadConversations RPC, 2–3 day estimates) when the user only asked whether pagination is needed.

   Scope:
   Coverage tooling is not configured in the project and cannot be enabled without setup work; User asks about coverage of a single file or a single test rather than the whole suite; A full test run is known to be prohibitively long and the user only wants an existing report read

   Check:
   Confirm the test run reports all tests passed and the coverage file's modification time is newer than the run start; check that covered + uncovered line

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Confirm the user's scoping: v1 needs no pagination — messages already use scroll+prefetch and group members are capped at 200; only add RPC/keyset pagination if/when an Inbox conversation-list UI is actually built.
  2. Prefer chaining shell commands with `;` on PowerShell; `&&` fails with 'The token && is not a valid statement separator'.
  3. Prefer reporting the actual branch name when it is not main/trunk, so the user knows where the commit landed.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid `git add .` or `git add -A` when the tree has unrelated modifications — it would sweep them into the commit.
  2. Avoid assuming `&&` works in the shell; the first attempt failed with a PowerShell parser error before being retried with `;`.
  3. Avoid claiming success without a post-commit `git show`/`git status` check that the commit touched exactly one file.

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   4. **الـ "Intentionally not changed"** — ده أهم سطر في التقرير. إنك سبت الـ serial push (idempotency > speed)، والـ N+1 الكامل، والـ entity layer — ده مش كسل، ده **تحديد أولويات صح**. الـ 20-row batch ما يستاهلش إعاد

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   رأيي الصريح — الشغل ده **هندسيًا ممتاز**، وفيه نضج واضح في قرار "إيه اللي ما نعملوش". بس فيه 3 نقاط لازم تتقفل قبل ما نعتبر الـ wave ده خلص.
   ## النقاط القوية
   
   1. **`SupabaseConfig` بالـ re-export shims** — ده أذكى من النقل المباشر. ما كسرتش الـ imports الموجودة، وفي نفس الوقت بقت **مصدر حقيقة واحد**. ده بالظبط روح الـ 4.4 بس بطريقة آمنة.
   
   2. **إصلاح `injection.dart` في pack_app** — ده كان **ثغرة أمنية مش بس تحسين**. الـ `Dio()` المباشر كان بيتخطى الـ apikey/auth/company/retry interceptors. دلوقتي الـ `DioFactory.create` بيضمن إن كل الـ middleware شغال. ده أهم تعديل في التقرير كله.
   
   3. **`COUNT(*)` بدل fetch-all-rows** — قبل كده كنت بتبعت الـ payloads كلها للـ UI عشان تحسب `length`. دلوقتي `selectOnly` أخف بكتير ومفيش data leak للـ UI.
   
   4. **الـ "Intentionally not changed"** — ده أهم سطر في التقرير. إنك سبت الـ serial push (idempotency > speed)، والـ N+1 الكامل، والـ entity layer — ده مش كسل، ده **تحديد أولويات صح**. الـ 20-row batch ما يستاهلش إعاد
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   2. **إصلاح `injection.dart` في pack_app** — ده كان **ثغرة أمنية مش بس تحسين**. الـ `Dio()` المباشر كان بيتخطى الـ apikey/auth/company/retry interceptors. دلوقتي الـ `DioFactory.create` بيضمن إن كل الـ middleware شغال. ده أهم تعديل في التقرير كله.
   
   3. **`COUNT(*)` بدل fetch-all-rows** — قبل كده كنت بتبعت الـ payloads كلها للـ UI عشان تحسب `length`. دلوقتي `selectOnly` أخف بكتير ومفيش data leak للـ UI.
   
   4. **الـ "Intentionally not changed"** — ده أهم سطر في التقرير. إنك سبت الـ serial push (idempotency > speed)، والـ N+1 الكامل، والـ entity layer — ده مش كسل، ده **تحديد أولويات صح**. الـ 20-row batch ما يستاهلش إعاد
   ...[truncated]

   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   4. **الـ "Intentionally not changed"** — ده أهم سطر في التقرير. إنك سبت الـ serial push (idempotency > speed)، والـ N+1 الكامل، والـ entity layer — ده مش كسل، ده **تحديد أولويات صح**. الـ 20-row batch ما يستاهلش إعاد

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

</current_user_request>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   **تأكيد واحد قبل التطبيق:** الـ `Idempotency-Key` في الـ header بيتطابق مع `id` اللي عليه الـ `on conflict`؟ الـ sanity #3 (dedup مرتين بنفس المفتاح → `200` بنفس `server_id` + `server_updated_at`) هو اللي هيثبت ده. لو الـ `200` التاني رجّع timestamp مختلف، قولي فوراً — ده معناه الـ `set_updated_at` trigger بيضرب الذرّية.
   
   ### القرار 2 — الـ totals: **معتمد كتسهيل موثّق**
   
   اقتباس العميل بيختار tier واحد (قطاعي أو جملة)، فالـ total الوحيد ده هو الصحيح. الـ 0 في العمود التاني مقبول دلوقتي لأن **الـ pull للـ quotes مش موج
   ...[truncated]

## Skill Memories


1. Skill
   id: skill_87ac8c549525002469f5

   Name:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

   Description:
   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   ...[truncated]

   monorepo_gated_push_closeout

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer bounded Start-Sleep + gh run list re-check; never gh run watch when a no-watch order applies.
  2. Prefer fresh gates in a clean detached worktree at the exact tip; cite prior fresh gates only for docs-only delta.
  3. Prefer git cherry merge-proofs before any worktree/branch deletion.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid deleting worktrees or branches without zero-unmerged git cherry proof and empty porcelain.
  2. Avoid pushing on stale gate results unless only docs-only delta and code tree byte-identical.
  3. Avoid pushing when porcelain is not empty or unpushed count differs from the expected number.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   - **مش** دفعة عمليات تحت مفتاح واحد. الـ `SyncEngine` بيبعت **واحدة واحدة**، وده المقصود: **مفتاح idempotency واحد = عملية واحدة**. لو جمعت عمليات تحت مفتاح واحد، الفشل الجزئي يبقى صعب التعامل معاه.
   - الـ `Idempotency-Key` و`X-Company-ID` **من الـ headers** (مش الـ body) — ده اللي `DioSyncTransport` (المهمة 5) بيعمله فعلاً. خليك متسق معاه.
   - الـ body = الـ payload: الـ `quote` + الـ `quote_items` متداخلين.
   - الـ function تكتب `quotes` + `quote_items` في **transaction واحد** (زي الذرّية المحلية بالظبط).
   - اعمل route على حسب حقل `type` عشان المستقبل، بس **نفّذ اللي محتاجينه دلوقتي بس** (`create_quote`). متبنيلهاش أنواع مش مستخدمة.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

   Trigger:

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

   Trigger:
   User requests a commit and/or push while the working tree contains modified or untracked files (git status --porcelain shows entries) and the current branch is known.

   Guidance:
   1) Run `git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` and `git status --porcelain=v1` to capture branch and pending changes. 2) Group related changes into logical commits and stage them with `git add <paths>`. 3) Commit each group with a descriptive message (`git commit -m "<type>(<scope>): <summary>"`). 4) Push the branch to its remote (`git push`). 5) If the user also asked to continue other work, resume that task after the push succeeds.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

   ...[truncated]

   - الـ `Idempotency-Key` و`X-Company-ID` **من الـ headers** (مش الـ body) — ده اللي `DioSyncTransport` (المهمة 5) بيعمله فعلاً. خليك متسق معاه.
   - الـ body = الـ payload: الـ `quote` + الـ `quote_items` متداخلين.
   - الـ function تكتب `quotes` + `quote_items` في **transaction واحد** (زي الذرّية المحلية بالظبط).
   - اعمل route على حسب حقل `type` عشان المستقبل، بس **نفّذ اللي محتاجينه دلوقتي بس** (`create_quote`). متبنيلهاش أنواع مش مستخدمة.
   
   ### 2. الرد عند الـ dedup: **`200` بالنتيجة الأصلية**
   ...[truncated]

   Trigger:
   User requests a commit and/or push while the working tree contains modified or untracked files (git status --porcelain shows entries) and the current branch is known.

   Guidance:
   1) Run `git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` and `git status --porcelain=v1` to capture branch and pending changes. 2) Group related changes into logical commits and stage them with `git add <paths>`. 3) Commit each group with a descriptive message (`git commit -m "<type>(<scope>): <summary>"`). 4) Push the branch to its remote (`git push`). 5) If the user also asked to continue other work, resume that task after the push succeeds.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

   Check:
   Re-run `git status --porcelain=v1` to confirm no unintended pending changes remain, and c
   ...[truncated]

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

   ## الخطوة 1 معتمدة ✅
   الفحص الأمني نضيف، والكوميتين صح. نقطة ذكية إنك جبت كود الـ Edge Functions من Supabase وكتبته محلياً في `supabase/functions/` — ده قفل خطر ضياع تاني فعلاً.
   
   ---
   
   ## إجابات الأسئلة الثلاثة
   
   ### 1. الشكل: **endpoint واحد، عملية واحدة لكل طلب**
   
   - **مش** دفعة عمليات تحت مفتاح واحد. الـ `SyncEngine` بيبعت **واحدة واحدة**، وده المقصود: **مفتاح idempotency واحد = عملية واحدة**. لو جمعت عمليات تحت مفتاح واحد، الفشل الجزئي يبقى صعب التعامل معاه.
   - الـ `Idempotency-Key` و`X-Company-ID` **من الـ headers** (مش الـ body) — ده اللي `DioSyncTransport` (المهمة 5) بيعمله فعلاً. خليك متسق معاه.
   - الـ body = الـ payload: الـ `quote` + الـ `quote_items` متداخلين.
   - الـ function تكتب `quotes` + `quote_items` في **transaction واحد** (زي الذرّية المحلية بالظبط).
   - اعمل route على حسب حقل `type` عشان المستقبل، بس **نفّذ اللي محتاجينه دلوقتي بس** (`create_quote`). متبنيلهاش أنواع مش مستخدمة.
   
   ### 2. الرد عند الـ dedup: **`200` بالنتيجة الأصلية**

## L2 Experience Memories


1. Candidate Experience (unverified)
   id: policy_4caffc4c2349515ce206

   Use:
   Candidate, unverified guidance. Treat it as a hypothesis and verify it in the current task before use.

   Trigger:
   User requests a commit and/or push while the working tree contains modified or untracked files (git status --porcelain shows entries) and the current branch is known.

   Guidance:
   1) Run `git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` and `git status --porcelain=v1` to capture branch and pending changes. 2) Group related changes into logical commits and stage them with `git add <paths>`. 3) Commit each group with a descriptive message (`git commit -m "<type>(<scope>): <summary>"`). 4) Push the branch to its remote (`git push`). 5) If the user also asked to continue other work, resume that task after the push succeeds.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

   Check:
   Re-run `git status --porcelain=v1` to confirm no unintended pending changes remain, and c
   ...[truncated]

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.

   Use:
   Candidate, unverified guidance. Treat it as a hypothesis and verify it in the current task before use.

   Trigger:
   User requests a commit and/or push while the working tree contains modified or untracked files (git status --porcelain shows entries) and the current branch is known.

   Guidance:
   1) Run `git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD` and `git status --porcelain=v1` to capture branch and pending changes. 2) Group related changes into logical commits and stage them with `git add <paths>`. 3) Commit each group with a descriptive message (`git commit -m "<type>(<scope>): <summary>"`). 4) Push the branch to its remote (`git push`). 5) If the user also asked to continue other work, resume that task after the push succeeds.

   Scope:
   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first

   Check:
   Re-run `git status --porcelain=v1` to confirm no unintended pending changes remain, and c
   ...[truncated]

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

   - الـ function تكتب `quotes` + `quote_items` في **transaction واحد** (زي الذرّية المحلية بالظبط).

## Decision guidance (distilled from past similar situations)

Apply these BEFORE choosing your next action. Each line was learned
from one or more past episodes where the user told us what to prefer
or avoid in this kind of context.

**Prefer**
  1. Prefer one batched shell invocation so fetch, commit list, gates, and status describe the same snapshot.
  2. Prefer re-running gates on the current tip rather than trusting gate lines quoted in the report.
  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

## Follow-up memory tools

If details are needed, use `memmy_memory_get(id)` with a non-User-Memory id above; User Memory entries are complete as shown.
Use `memmy_memory_search(query)` only when the recalled memory is insufficient or ambiguous.
</memmy_memory_context>

  3. Prefer reading artifacts (JSON, APK/aapt output) directly when the report cites them.

**Avoid**
  1. Avoid accepting a report's commit count or gate results without reconciling them against observed output.
  2. Avoid pushing as part of verification unless the task explicitly authorizes it.
  3. Avoid treating a missing or skipped gate as a pass; mark it unverified.

   Working tree is clean and there is nothing to commit; User only asked to inspect status or history, not to commit or push; Repository is in a detached HEAD or mid-rebase/merge state requiring conflict resolution first
